- **multi-expert-analyzer**: 针对通用问题进行多领域专家联合分析, 综合稿产生前必经 fact-checker 与 red-team 两道独立校验。适用场景: 用户提出跨领域或不确定领域的复杂问题, 需要从多个专家角度分别搜证并相互校验后综合成文, 例如该不该买房、该不该跳槽、是否进入某个赛道等。触发关键词: 多角度分析、专家分析、综合分析、多视角、跨领域分析、从不同角度看、深度分析。问题只属于单一明确领域时, 优先使用该领域的专门 skill, 例如纯财务用 finance-core-analysis、纯技术用 software-architect。输出 (全部 markdown 保存到当前项目 markdown/ 目录): (1) 每位专家的中间分析稿; (2) 事实核查报告 (跨专家矛盾 / 未经证实的主张 / 重叠区); (3) 红队反驳报告 (逐条反驳承重墙级主张, 给出 SURVIVES / WEAKENS / REFUTED 裁定); (4) 第一人称、通俗易懂的最终综合长文, 同时报告哪些主张通过验证、多少被弱化、多少被剔除。 ([digoal/blog](https://github.com/digoal/blog/tree/HEAD/skills/skills_archive/multi-expert-analyzer_20260706) · ★8.6k)

- **multi-expert-analyzer**: 多领域专家联合分析 skill，深度思考分析解答任何领域的棘手问题。流程：主控 agent 识别问题所属领域，动态调度 1~N 个领域专家并行搜证、分析、产稿，再交由事实核查员与红队进行克制温和的二次审查，最终综合成第一人称、面向小白、文笔一气呵成的 markdown 文章。当用户提出跨界、复杂、有争议的硬问题，希望从多视角深度拆解；或明确要求"深度分析 / 多角度分析 / 专家会诊 / 多领域联合分析"时触发。 ([digoal/blog](https://github.com/digoal/blog/tree/HEAD/skills/skills_archive/multi-expert-analyzer_20260705) · ★8.6k)
- **multi-expert-analyzer**: 对任何领域的棘手问题进行深度思考分析,先识别问题所属领域并按需调度多位领域专家并行作答, 之后由事实核查员和红队做克制的事实审计与对抗性反驳,最后整合为面向小白的第一人称终稿。 适用:用户抛出一个跨领域/有深度的真实问题,希望听到多位专家的视角、看到观点的边界与证伪条件, 并得到一气呵成、通俗有据的最终文章。不适用:简单事实查询、纯情绪吐槽、不要求证据的随口一问。 ([digoal/blog](https://github.com/digoal/blog/tree/HEAD/skills/skills_archive/multi-expert-analyzer_20260708) · ★8.6k)

- **multi-method-benchmark-table-reproduction**: Use when replicating a published single-cell clustering benchmark: multiple clustering methods must be run on the same pre-annotated scRNA-seq matrices with known cluster counts and NMI/ARI assembled into a comparison table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-method-benchmark-table-reproduction))
- **multi-method-benchmark-visualization**: Use when comparing TR-identification methods on the same epigenomic query region sets and a known ground-truth perturbed TR exists, so ranks can be converted into mean reciprocal rank values for head-to-head benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/multi-method-benchmark-visualization))
- **multi-method-benchmark-visualization**: Use when comparing a new trajectory inference method's pseudotime accuracy against baselines on simulated single-cell datasets with ground truth, using Kendall rank correlation as the metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-method-benchmark-visualization))
- **multi-method-bin-count-aggregation**: Use when benchmarking a viral binning method against baselines on assembled metagenomic Hi-C data and you need per-method counts of high/medium-completion bins (CheckV) to quantify improvement percentages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/multi-method-bin-count-aggregation))
- **multi-method-cci-consensus-voting**: Use when you need reliable positive/negative labels for candidate L-R interactions between cell clusters from a labeled scRNA-seq matrix (e.g. panc8) to supervise a deep-learning CCI model, and no single statistical method's calls should be trusted alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-method-cci-consensus-voting))
- **multi-method-cell-cell-communication-benchmarking**: Use when comparing performance of cell-cell communication inference tools (CellChat, CellPhoneDB, iTALK, SingleCellSignalR) on identical scRNA-seq datasets to quantify false-positive rates and robustness to cell subsampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-method-cell-cell-communication-benchmarking))
- **multi-method-cell-type-benchmarking**: Use when you have single-cell count matrices with manually annotated labels and need to quantitatively compare which automatic cell-type identification methods (eager, lazy or marker-based) perform , e.g. across platforms or tissues. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-method-cell-type-benchmarking))
- **multi-method-clustering-benchmark-comparison**: Use when comparing several single-cell clustering methods on the same scRNA-seq datasets and you need quantitative stability (APN) and tightness (silhouette width) metrics for a benchmark table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-method-clustering-benchmark-comparison))
- **multi-method-clustering-boxplot-visualization**: Use when a task requires comparing clustering quality of scSpace vs baselines (Louvain, K-means, Hclust, SpaGCN, STAGATE, BayesSpace, DR-SC, scCoGAPS) on labeled simulated datasets via ARI boxplots. - **multi-method-clustering-pipeline-execution**: Use when clustering the 15 Krzak et al. 2019 real scRNA-seq evaluation datasets under R 4.0.5 and comparing CASCC against CIDR, RaceID3, SC3, Seurat V4, SIMLR, and TSCAN using ARI/AMI/NMI against ground-truth cell-type labels. - **multi-method-comparison-table-compilation**: Use when a gold-standard dataset with known branching topology (e.g., Rizvi et al. synthetic data with two bifurcations) is available and the goal is to rank multiple trajectory-inference methods on bifurcation detection and pseudotime accuracy. - **multi-method-deg-consensus-voting**: Use when a raw RNA-seq count table plus sample metadata must be screened for DEGs and no single method can be trusted, e.g. rare-disease model datasets like PMM2-CDG or Lafora disease where method choice strongly changes DEG counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-method-deg-consensus-voting))
- **multi-method-enrichment-comparison**: Use when evaluating or comparing pathway enrichment methods on gene/KO-level omics data with known ground-truth target pathways (knockout genes or disease pathways), e.g. reproducing Figure 2C-D sensitivity/specificity or ranking benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multi-method-enrichment-comparison))
